Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in geological theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 668 and the invariant I(A,B) = 965 mod 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_668(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 668. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^965(S, F) is trivial. QED.
